> # 🗣️ [회화] 오성식 생활영어 7200 책 (Part: 전체)
>
> ## 🎯 학습 개요 (Learning Overview)
> - **주요 학습 테마**: 일상적인 인사, 안부 묻기, 작별 인사, 감사 및 사과 표현, 건강 상태, 여가 활동(영화, 스포츠, 음주, 춤), 운전 및 교통 표지판 이해
> - **핵심 수록 표현 수**: 1030개 (대화 및 개별 표현 포함)
> - **학습 포인트**:
> - 다양한 상황에서 자연스럽게 사용되는 인사와 작별 표현을 익힙니다.
> - 감정 표현, 건강 상태, 취미 생활에 대한 대화를 통해 실용적인 어휘와 문장 구조를 학습합니다.
> - 원어민이 자주 사용하는 구어체 표현과 뉘앙스를 파악하여 실제 대화 능력을 향상시킵니다.
>
> ## 📚 무손실 핵심 표현 & 패턴 마스터 (Core Expressions & Patterns)
>
> ### 🔹 1. `How are you doing?` : 어떻게 지내세요?
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 가장 흔하고 일반적인 안부 인사입니다. `How are you?`와 거의 동일하게 사용되며, 좀 더 캐주얼한 느낌을 줍니다.
> - **🔊 발음 & 연음 팁 (Pronunciation Tip)**: `How-are-you-doing`이 자연스럽게 `How-are-ya-doing`처럼 들릴 수 있습니다. `doing`의 `g`는 약하게 발음되거나 생략되기도 합니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `Hi, Mark. How are you doing?`
> - 🇰🇷 안녕, 마크. 어떻게 지내?
> - 🇺🇸 `I'm fine. Thank you. And you?`
> - 🇰🇷 잘 지내. 고마워. 너는?
>
> ### 🔹 2. `What's up?` : 무슨 일 있어? / 잘 지내?
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 매우 비격식적인 인사로, 친구나 가까운 사람에게 사용합니다. "별일 없어?" 또는 "잘 지내?"의 의미로 쓰입니다.
> - **🔊 발음 & 연음 팁 (Pronunciation Tip)**: `What's up`은 `왓썹`처럼 들립니다. `t` 소리가 약화되거나 생략됩니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `What's up?`
> - 🇰🇷 무슨 일 있어? / 잘 지내?
> - 🇺🇸 `Nothing much.`
> - 🇰🇷 별일 없어.
>
> ### 🔹 3. `How's everything with you?` : 모든 일은 어때?
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 상대방의 전반적인 상황이나 안부를 묻는 표현입니다. `How are you doing?`보다 조금 더 포괄적인 느낌을 줍니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `How's everything with you?`
> - 🇰🇷 모든 일은 어때?
> - 🇺🇸 `Just so so.`
> - 🇰🇷 그저 그래.
>
> ### 🔹 4. `How are you feeling today?` : 오늘 기분은 어때? / 몸은 좀 어때?
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 주로 상대방의 건강 상태나 기분을 물을 때 사용합니다. 특히 이전에 아팠던 사람에게 묻기 좋습니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `How are you feeling today?`
> - 🇰🇷 오늘 기분은 어때? / 몸은 좀 어때?
> - 🇺🇸 `Much better than yesterday.`
> - 🇰🇷 어제보다 훨씬 나아.
>
> ### 🔹 5. `Have you been taking medicine?` : 약은 복용하고 있었니?
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 과거부터 현재까지 약을 계속 복용하고 있는지 묻는 현재완료 진행형 질문입니다. 상대방의 건강 상태에 대한 관심과 염려를 나타냅니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `Have you been taking medicine?`
> - 🇰🇷 약은 복용하고 있었니?
> - 🇺🇸 `Yes, everyday.`
> - 🇰🇷 응, 매일.
>
> ### 🔹 6. `Hi, there!` : 안녕! (거기!)
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 친근하게 누군가를 부르거나 인사할 때 사용합니다. 특정 대상을 지칭하기보다 가볍게 인사를 건넬 때 좋습니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `Hi, there!`
> - 🇰🇷 안녕! (거기!)
> - 🇺🇸 `How are ya(you)? Long time no see.`
> - 🇰🇷 잘 지내? 오랜만이야.
>
> ### 🔹 7. `Long time no see.` : 오랜만이야.
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 오랫동안 만나지 못했던 사람에게 사용하는 매우 흔하고 비격식적인 인사입니다. 문법적으로 완벽하진 않지만 관용적으로 널리 쓰입니다.
> - **🔊 발음 & 연음 팁 (Pronunciation Tip)**: `Long time no see`는 한 덩어리로 빠르게 발음됩니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `How are ya(you)? Long time no see.`
> - 🇰🇷 잘 지내? 오랜만이야.
> - 🇺🇸 `It seems like years.`
> - 🇰🇷 몇 년은 된 것 같아.
>
> ### 🔹 8. `What's new with you?` : 무슨 새로운 일 있어?
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 상대방에게 최근에 있었던 특별한 일이나 소식을 묻는 표현입니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `What's new with you?`
> - 🇰🇷 무슨 새로운 일 있어?
> - 🇺🇸 `I can't complain.`
> - 🇰🇷 불평할 거 없어. (나쁘지 않아.)
>
> ### 🔹 9. `I can't complain.` : 불평할 거 없어. (나쁘지 않아.)
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: "잘 지내?" 등의 질문에 대한 긍정적인 답변으로, "아주 좋지는 않지만, 딱히 불평할 만한 나쁜 일도 없다"는 뉘앙스입니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `What's new with you?`
> - 🇰🇷 무슨 새로운 일 있어?
> - 🇺🇸 `I can't complain.`
> - 🇰🇷 불평할 거 없어.
>
> ### 🔹 10. `Have you gotten over your cold?` : 감기는 다 나았니?
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: `get over`는 질병이나 어려움에서 회복하다는 의미입니다. 과거에 앓았던 감기에서 완전히 회복했는지 묻는 질문입니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `Have you gotten over your cold?`
> - 🇰🇷 감기는 다 나았니?
> - 🇺🇸 `Not yet.`
> - 🇰🇷 아직 아니.
>
> ### 🔹 11. `Why don't you get some rest?` : 좀 쉬는 게 어때?
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 상대방에게 ~하는 것이 좋겠다는 제안이나 권유를 할 때 사용하는 표현입니다. `Why don't you...?`는 부드러운 제안입니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `Why don't you get some rest?`
> - 🇰🇷 좀 쉬는 게 어때?
> - 🇺🇸 `I'd like to, but I can't.`
> - 🇰🇷 그러고 싶지만, 그럴 수 없어.
>
> ### 🔹 12. `What are you doing these days?` : 요즘 뭐 하고 지내?
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 상대방의 최근 근황이나 활동을 묻는 일반적인 질문입니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `What are you doing these days?`
> - 🇰🇷 요즘 뭐 하고 지내?
> - 🇺🇸 `I have been very busy with my new project.`
> - 🇰🇷 새 프로젝트 때문에 아주 바빴어.
>
> ### 🔹 13. `I've been up late the past couple of nights.` : 지난 며칠 밤늦게까지 깨어 있었어.
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: `be up late`는 밤늦게까지 깨어 있다는 의미입니다. `the past couple of nights`는 지난 2~3일 밤을 의미합니다. 피곤한 이유를 설명할 때 사용합니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `You look very tired.`
> - 🇰🇷 너 아주 피곤해 보여.
> - 🇺🇸 `I've been up late the past couple of nights.`
> - 🇰🇷 지난 며칠 밤늦게까지 깨어 있었어.
>
> ### 14. `I didn't expect to see you here.` : 여기서 널 볼 줄은 몰랐어.
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 예상치 못한 장소에서 아는 사람을 만났을 때 놀라움을 표현하는 말입니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `I didn't expect to see you here.`
> - 🇰🇷 여기서 널 볼 줄은 몰랐어.
> - 🇺🇸 `I just stopped by to say hello.`
> - 🇰🇷 그냥 안부 인사하려고 잠깐 들렀어.
>
> ### 🔹 15. `I just stopped by to say hello.` : 그냥 안부 인사하려고 잠깐 들렀어.
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: `stop by`는 잠깐 들르다, 방문하다는 의미의 구동사입니다. 특별한 목적 없이 가볍게 방문했음을 나타냅니다.
> - **🔊 발음 & 연음 팁 (Pronunciation Tip)**: `stopped by`는 `스탑트 바이`가 아닌 `스탑 바이`처럼 `p`와 `b`가 연음되어 발음됩니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `I didn't expect to see you here.`
> - 🇰🇷 여기서 널 볼 줄은 몰랐어.
> - 🇺🇸 `I just stopped by to say hello.`
> - 🇰🇷 그냥 안부 인사하려고 잠깐 들렀어.
>
> ### 🔹 16. `Is everything okay with you?` : 너 괜찮아? / 별일 없어?
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 상대방의 안녕이나 상황에 문제가 없는지 확인하는 질문입니다. 걱정이나 염려를 담을 수 있습니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `Is everything okay with you?`
> - 🇰🇷 너 괜찮아? / 별일 없어?
> - 🇺🇸 `Yes, everything's fine.`
> - 🇰🇷 응, 모든 게 괜찮아.
>
> ### 🔹 17. `Why are you in such a good mood?` : 왜 그렇게 기분이 좋아?
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 상대방의 좋은 기분을 알아채고 그 이유를 물을 때 사용합니다. `in a good mood`는 기분이 좋다는 뜻입니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `Why are you in such a good mood?`
> - 🇰🇷 왜 그렇게 기분이 좋아?
> - 🇺🇸 `Because I'm happy about my new job.`
> - 🇰🇷 새 직장 때문에 기뻐서.
>
> ### 🔹 18. `What have you been up to lately?` : 요즘 뭐 하고 지냈어?
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: `What have you been up to?`는 `What have you been doing?`과 유사하게 최근 근황을 묻는 비격식적인 표현입니다. `lately`는 '최근에'라는 뜻입니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `What have you been up to lately?`
> - 🇰🇷 요즘 뭐 하고 지냈어?
> - 🇺🇸 `Just fooling around.`
> - 🇰🇷 그냥 빈둥거렸어.
>
> ### 🔹 19. `Just fooling around.` : 그냥 빈둥거렸어.
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: `fool around`는 특별한 목적 없이 시간을 보내거나 장난치며 빈둥거린다는 의미입니다. 캐주얼한 상황에서 사용합니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `What have you been up to lately?`
> - 🇰🇷 요즘 뭐 하고 지냈어?
> - 🇺🇸 `Just fooling around.`
> - 🇰🇷 그냥 빈둥거렸어.
>
> ### 🔹 20. `You are the last person I would have expected to see here.` : 여기서 널 보게 될 줄은 정말 상상도 못 했어.
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 예상치 못한 장소에서 누군가를 만났을 때, 그 사람이 가장 마지막으로 예상했던 사람이라는 것을 강조하여 놀라움을 표현합니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `John! You are the last person I would have expected to see here. I heard you had gone back to the States.`
> - 🇰🇷 존! 여기서 널 보게 될 줄은 정말 상상도 못 했어. 미국으로 돌아갔다고 들었는데.
> - 🇺🇸 `Who told you that?`
> - 🇰🇷 누가 그렇게 말했어?
>
> ### 🔹 21. `It seems like years.` : 몇 년은 된 것 같아.
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: `Long time no see`에 대한 응답으로, 실제로 몇 년이 지났거나, 시간이 매우 길게 느껴졌다는 것을 강조할 때 사용합니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `Hi, long time no see.`
> - 🇰🇷 안녕, 오랜만이야.
> - 🇺🇸 `It seems like years.`
> - 🇰🇷 몇 년은 된 것 같아.
>
> ### 🔹 22. `I've been trying to find you.` : 널 찾으려고 노력했어.
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 오랫동안 연락이 닿지 않았던 사람에게 그동안 찾고 있었다는 것을 표현하여 반가움을 나타냅니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `I know. I've been trying to find you.`
> - 🇰🇷 알아. 널 찾으려고 노력했어.
> - 🇺🇸 `Have you? I've been thinking about you a lot, too.`
> - 🇰🇷 그랬어? 나도 널 많이 생각했어.
>
> ### 🔹 23. `Do you know what ever happened to Pam?` : 팸에게 무슨 일이 있었는지 알아?
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 오랫동안 소식이 끊긴 사람에게 무슨 일이 있었는지 궁금해하며 묻는 표현입니다. `ever`는 '도대체'라는 강조의 의미를 더합니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `Do you know what ever happened to Pam?`
> - 🇰🇷 팸에게 무슨 일이 있었는지 알아?
> - 🇺🇸 `I haven't heard from her in years.`
> - 🇰🇷 몇 년 동안 그녀에게서 소식을 못 들었어.
>
> ### 🔹 24. `I haven't heard from her in years.` : 몇 년 동안 그녀에게서 소식을 못 들었어.
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: `hear from someone`은 ~로부터 소식을 듣다, 연락을 받다는 의미입니다. 오랫동안 연락이 없었음을 나타냅니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `Do you know what ever happened to Pam?`
> - 🇰🇷 팸에게 무슨 일이 있었는지 알아?
> - 🇺🇸 `I haven't heard from her in years.`
> - 🇰🇷 몇 년 동안 그녀에게서 소식을 못 들었어.
>
> ### 🔹 25. `I bet she is.` : 분명 그럴 거야. / 틀림없이 그럴 거야.
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: `I bet`은 '나는 ~라고 확신한다'는 의미로, 상대방의 말에 동의하며 강한 확신을 표현할 때 사용합니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `I hope she is doing okay.`
> - 🇰🇷 그녀가 잘 지내길 바라.
> - 🇺🇸 `I bet she is. We don't need to worry.`
> - 🇰🇷 분명 그럴 거야. 우린 걱정할 필요 없어.
>
> ### 🔹 26. `How's everybody at your house?` : 집에 있는 모든 사람들은 어때?
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 상대방의 가족 전체의 안부를 묻는 표현입니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `How's everybody at your house?`
> - 🇰🇷 집에 있는 모든 사람들은 어때?
> - 🇺🇸 `My family's fine. How about yours?`
> - 🇰🇷 우리 가족은 잘 지내. 네 가족은 어때?
>
> ### 🔹 27. `Everybody's doing fine except my Mom.` : 엄마 빼고는 모두 잘 지내.
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: `except`은 '~을 제외하고'라는 뜻입니다. 특정 사람을 제외한 모두의 안부를 전할 때 사용합니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `My family's fine. How about yours?`
> - 🇰🇷 우리 가족은 잘 지내. 네 가족은 어때?
> - 🇺🇸 `Everybody's doing fine except my Mom.`
> - 🇰🇷 엄마 빼고는 모두 잘 지내.
>
> ### 🔹 28. `She has been ill for the past week.` : 그녀는 지난주부터 아팠어.
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: `for the past week`는 '지난주부터 지금까지'라는 의미로, 현재완료 시제와 함께 사용하여 과거부터 현재까지 지속된 상태를 나타냅니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `Oh, what's wrong with her?`
> - 🇰🇷 아, 그녀에게 무슨 일 있어?
> - 🇺🇸 `She has been ill for the past week.`
> - 🇰🇷 그녀는 지난주부터 아팠어.
>
> ### 🔹 29. `It was fantastic. I did so much.` : 환상적이었어. 정말 많은 것을 했어.
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 여행이나 경험에 대한 긍정적인 감상을 표현할 때 사용합니다. `fantastic`은 '환상적인, 아주 좋은'이라는 뜻입니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `How was your trip?`
> - 🇰🇷 여행은 어땠어?
> - 🇺🇸 `It was fantastic. I did so much.`
> - 🇰🇷 환상적이었어. 정말 많은 것을 했어.
>
> ### 🔹 30. `How do you do? I'm glad to meet you.` : 처음 뵙겠습니다. 만나서 반갑습니다.
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 격식 있는 첫 만남에서 사용하는 인사입니다. `How do you do?`는 질문이지만, 보통 답변도 `How do you do?`로 합니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `How do you do? I'm glad to meet you.`
> - 🇰🇷 처음 뵙겠습니다. 만나서 반갑습니다.
> - 🇺🇸 `Pleased to meet you, too.`
> - 🇰🇷 저도 만나서 반갑습니다.
>
> ### 🔹 31. `Let me introduce myself.` : 제 소개를 하겠습니다.
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 자기소개를 시작할 때 사용하는 격식 있는 표현입니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `Let me introduce myself. My name is Mrs. Smith.`
> - 🇰🇷 제 소개를 하겠습니다. 제 이름은 스미스 부인입니다.
> - 🇺🇸 `I'm Mr. Johnes.`
> - 🇰🇷 저는 존스 씨입니다.
>
> ### 🔹 32. `Where are you from?` : 어디 출신이세요?
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 상대방의 출신 국가나 지역을 묻는 기본적인 질문입니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `Where are you from?`
> - 🇰🇷 어디 출신이세요?
> - 🇺🇸 `You mean which country I'm from?`
> - 🇰🇷 제가 어느 나라 출신인지 말씀이세요?
>
> ### 🔹 33. `Let me introduce my friend, Pam.` : 제 친구 팸을 소개하겠습니다.
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 다른 사람을 소개할 때 사용하는 표현입니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `Let me introduce my friend, Pam.`
> - 🇰🇷 제 친구 팸을 소개하겠습니다.
> - 🇺🇸 `I think I've seen her before.`
> - 🇰🇷 전에 그녀를 본 것 같아요.
>
> ### 🔹 34. `Do I know you from somewhere?` : 어디선가 당신을 아는 것 같은데요?
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 상대방이 낯이 익을 때, 이전에 만난 적이 있는지 확인하는 질문입니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `Do I know you from somewhere?`
> - 🇰🇷 어디선가 당신을 아는 것 같은데요?
> - 🇺🇸 `Maybe. You look very familiar.`
> - 🇰🇷 아마도요. 아주 낯이 익네요.
>
> ### 🔹 35. `You look very familiar.` : 아주 낯이 익네요.
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 상대방의 얼굴이 익숙하게 느껴질 때 사용하는 표현입니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `Do I know you from somewhere?`
> - 🇰🇷 어디선가 당신을 아는 것 같은데요?
> - 🇺🇸 `Maybe. You look very familiar.`
> - 🇰🇷 아마도요. 아주 낯이 익네요.
>
> ### 🔹 36. `I remember now. We met at a party.` : 이제 기억나요. 우리 파티에서 만났죠.
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 잊고 있던 기억이 떠올랐을 때 사용하는 표현입니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `I remember now. We met at a party.`
> - 🇰🇷 이제 기억나요. 우리 파티에서 만났죠.
> - 🇺🇸 `That's right. At Tom's party.`
> - 🇰🇷 맞아요. 톰의 파티에서요.
>
> ### 🔹 37. `What should I call you?` : 뭐라고 불러야 할까요?
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 상대방을 어떻게 불러야 할지 모를 때 정중하게 묻는 질문입니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `What should I call you?`
> - 🇰🇷 뭐라고 불러야 할까요?
> - 🇺🇸 `Please call me by my first name.`
> - 🇰🇷 제 이름으로 불러주세요.
>
> ### 🔹 38. `Please call me by my first name.` : 제 이름으로 불러주세요.
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 상대방에게 성이 아닌 이름으로 불러달라고 요청할 때 사용합니다. 이는 좀 더 친근하게 대해달라는 의미를 내포합니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `What should I call you?`
> - 🇰🇷 뭐라고 불러야 할까요?
> - 🇺🇸 `Please call me by my first name.`
> - 🇰🇷 제 이름으로 불러주세요.
>
> ### 🔹 39. `I must have you mixed up with someone else.` : 다른 사람과 착각했나 봐요.
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 누군가를 다른 사람으로 착각했을 때 사과하며 사용하는 표현입니다. `mix up with`는 ~와 혼동하다, 착각하다는 의미입니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `I'm sorry. I must have you mixed up with someone else.`
> - 🇰🇷 죄송합니다. 다른 사람과 착각했나 봐요.
>
> ### 🔹 40. `How many are there in your family?` : 가족이 몇 명이에요?
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 상대방의 가족 구성원 수를 묻는 질문입니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `How many are there in your family?`
> - 🇰🇷 가족이 몇 명이에요?
> - 🇺🇸 `Five.`
> - 🇰🇷 다섯 명이에요.
>
> ### 🔹 41. `What does your father do for a living?` : 아버지는 무슨 일을 하세요?
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 상대방의 가족 구성원의 직업을 묻는 질문입니다. `for a living`은 '생계를 위해'라는 의미로, 직업을 물을 때 자연스럽게 사용됩니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `What does your father do for a living?`
> - 🇰🇷 아버지는 무슨 일을 하세요?
> - 🇺🇸 `He works for the government.`
> - 🇰🇷 그는 공무원입니다.
>
> ### 🔹 42. `Do you mind if I ask your age?` : 나이를 여쭤봐도 괜찮을까요?
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 상대방의 나이를 묻는 것은 실례가 될 수 있으므로, `Do you mind if I...?`를 사용하여 정중하게 허락을 구하는 표현입니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `Do you mind if I ask your age?`
> - 🇰🇷 나이를 여쭤봐도 괜찮을까요?
> - 🇺🇸 `Guess how old I am.`
> - 🇰🇷 제가 몇 살인지 맞춰보세요.
>
> ### 🔹 43. `Guess how old I am.` : 제가 몇 살인지 맞춰보세요.
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 나이를 직접 말하기보다 상대방에게 맞춰보라고 권유할 때 사용합니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `Do you mind if I ask your age?`
> - 🇰🇷 나이를 여쭤봐도 괜찮을까요?
> - 🇺🇸 `Guess how old I am.`
> - 🇰🇷 제가 몇 살인지 맞춰보세요.
>
> ### 🔹 44. `It's really hard to tell.` : 정말 알기 어렵네요.
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 상대방의 나이를 추측하기 어렵거나, 실례가 될까 봐 직접적으로 말하기 어려울 때 사용하는 표현입니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `Guess how old I am.`
> - 🇰🇷 제가 몇 살인지 맞춰보세요.
> - 🇺🇸 `It's really hard to tell.`
> - 🇰🇷 정말 알기 어렵네요.
>
> ### 🔹 45. `Just give it a try.` : 그냥 한번 시도해 보세요.
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 상대방에게 망설이지 말고 한번 해보라고 격려할 때 사용합니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `It's really hard to tell.`
> - 🇰🇷 정말 알기 어렵네요.
> - 🇺🇸 `Just give it a try. How old do I look?`
> - 🇰🇷 그냥 한번 시도해 보세요. 제가 몇 살로 보여요?
>
> ### 🔹 46. `Where are you working these days?` : 요즘 어디서 일하세요?
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 상대방의 현재 직장을 묻는 질문입니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `Where are you working these days?`
> - 🇰🇷 요즘 어디서 일하세요?
> - 🇺🇸 `I'm working at the Bank of Korea.`
> - 🇰🇷 한국은행에서 일하고 있어요.
>
> ### 🔹 47. `I've heard a great deal about that bank.` : 그 은행에 대해 많이 들었어요.
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 특정 대상에 대해 많은 정보를 들었거나 소문을 들었을 때 사용하는 표현입니다. `a great deal`은 '많은 양'을 의미합니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `I'm working at the Bank of Korea.`
> - 🇰🇷 한국은행에서 일하고 있어요.
> - 🇺🇸 `I've heard a great deal about that bank.`
> - 🇰🇷 그 은행에 대해 많이 들었어요.
>
> ### 🔹 48. `What was your first impression of Seoul?` : 서울의 첫인상은 어땠어요?
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 특정 장소나 사람에 대한 첫인상을 묻는 질문입니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `What was your first impression of Seoul?`
> - 🇰🇷 서울의 첫인상은 어땠어요?
> - 🇺🇸 `The mountains are beautiful and people are friendly.`
> - 🇰🇷 산들이 아름답고 사람들이 친절해요.
>
> ### 🔹 49. `What don't you like about Seoul?` : 서울에서 무엇이 마음에 들지 않아요?
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 특정 대상의 부정적인 측면이나 불만족스러운 점을 묻는 질문입니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `What don't you like about Seoul?`
> - 🇰🇷 서울에서 무엇이 마음에 들지 않아요?
> - 🇺🇸 `Traffic is terrible and it's too crowded.`
> - 🇰🇷 교통 체증이 심하고 너무 혼잡해요.
>
> ### 🔹 50. `Have you ever been to America?` : 미국에 가본 적 있으세요?
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 상대방의 과거 경험을 묻는 현재완료 시제 질문입니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `Have you ever been to America?`
> - 🇰🇷 미국에 가본 적 있으세요?
> - 🇺🇸 `No, not yet. But I would like to go visit my sister soon.`
> - 🇰🇷 아니요, 아직요. 하지만 곧 여동생을 방문하고 싶어요.
>
> ### 🔹 51. `Which part of America does she live in?` : 그녀는 미국 어디에 살아요?
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 특정 장소의 구체적인 위치를 묻는 질문입니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `Which part of America does she live in?`
> - 🇰🇷 그녀는 미국 어디에 살아요?
> - 🇺🇸 `In L.A.`
> - 🇰🇷 로스앤젤레스에요.
>
> ### 🔹 52. `It was a lovely party.` : 멋진 파티였어요.
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 파티나 모임이 좋았다는 감상을 표현할 때 사용합니다. `lovely`는 '아름다운, 멋진'이라는 뜻입니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `It was a lovely party.`
> - 🇰🇷 멋진 파티였어요.
> - 🇺🇸 `Please give my regards to your family.`
> - 🇰🇷 가족들에게 안부 전해주세요.
>
> ### 🔹 53. `Please give my regards to your family.` : 가족들에게 안부 전해주세요.
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 상대방의 가족이나 다른 사람에게 안부를 전해달라고 부탁할 때 사용하는 정중한 표현입니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `It was a lovely party.`
> - 🇰🇷 멋진 파티였어요.
> - 🇺🇸 `Please give my regards to your family.`
> - 🇰🇷 가족들에게 안부 전해주세요.
>
> ### 🔹 54. `Drive carefully.` : 조심히 운전하세요.
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 헤어질 때 상대방의 안전을 기원하며 하는 인사입니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `Okay. See you tomorrow at work.`
> - 🇰🇷 알겠습니다. 내일 직장에서 봐요.
> - 🇺🇸 `Drive carefully.`
> - 🇰🇷 조심히 운전하세요.
>
> ### 🔹 55. `Please excuse me, I'd better go now.` : 실례합니다만, 이제 가봐야겠어요.
> - **💡 뉘앙스 & 상황 맥락**: 자리를 떠야 할 때 정중하게 양해를 구하고 작별을 고하는 표현입니다. `I'd better go`는 '가는 게 좋겠다'는 의미입니다.
> - **📝 실전 예문 (Real Examples)**:
> - 🇺🇸 `Please excuse me, I'd better go now.`
> - 🇰🇷 실례합니다만, 이제 가봐야겠어요.

---

SECTION 1

GREETING

{{오성식생활영어1}}

Dialogue 1

a. Hi, John.

b. Hi, Mark. How are you doing?

a. I'm fine. Thank you. And you?

b. Fine, thanks.

Dialogue 2

a. What's up?

b. Nothing much.

a. How's everything with you?

b. Just so so.

Dialogue 3

a. How are you feeling today?

b. Much better than yesterday.

a. Have you been taking medicine?

b. Yes, everyday.

Dialogue 4

a. Hi, there!

b. How are ya(you)? Long time no see.

a. What's new with you?

b. I can't complain.

Dialogue 5

a. Have you gotten over your cold?

b. Not yet.

a. Why don't you get some rest?

b. I'd like to, but I can't.

Dialogue 6

a. What are you doing these days?

b. I have been very busy with my new

project.

a. You look very tired.

b. I've been up late the past couple of nights

Dialogue 7

a. What did you do last weekend?

b. I went to a baseball game.

a. How was it?

b. It was great. Our team won.

Dialogue 8

a. I didn't expect to see you here.

b. I just stopped by to say hello.

a. Is everything okay with you?

b. Yes, everything's fine.

a. Are you sure?

b. Yes, I'm sure.

Dialogue 9

a. Why are you in such a good mood?

b. Because I'm happy about my new job.

a. When did you get it?

b. They called me just before I came here.

Dialogue 10

a. What have you been up to lately?

b. Just fooling around.

a. Really? Like what?

b. You know the same old thing.

Dialogue 11

a. John! You are the last person I would

have  expected to see here. I heard

you had gone back to the States.

b. Who told you that?

Dialogue 12

a. Hi, long time no see.

b. It seems like years.

a. I know. I've been trying to find you.

b. Have you? I've been thinking about you

a lot, too.

Dialogue 13

a. Do you know what ever happened to

Pam?

b. I haven't heard from her in years.

a. I hope she is doing okay.

b. I bet she is. We don't need to worry.

Dialogue 14

a. How's everybody at your house?

b. My family's fine. How about yours?

a. Everybody's doing fine except my Mom.

b. Oh, what's wrong with her?

a. She has been ill for the past week.

b. Did she go see a doctor?

Dialogue 15

a. How was your trip?

b. It was fantastic. I did so much.

a. What did you do? Anything special?

b. Everything about the place was great.

SECTION 2

FIRST GREETINGS

Dialogue 16

a. How do you do? I'm glad to meet you.

b. Pleased to meet you, too.

a. Let me introduce myself.

My name is Mrs. Smith.

b. I'm Mr. Johnes.

Dialogue 17

a. Where are you from?

b. You mean which country I'm from?

a. Yes, I'm sorry.

b. No problem. I'm from Korea.

Dialogue 18

a. Let me introduce my friend, Pam.

b. I think I've seen her before.

a. Oh yeah? Where?

b. Let me think. Ah yes, in one of

my classes.

Dialogue 19

a. Do I know you from somewhere?

b. Maybe. You look very familiar.

a. I remember now. We met at a party.

b. That's right. At Tom's party.

Dialogue 20

a. What should I call you?

b. Please call me by my first name.

a. How do you pronounce it?

b. Sung-jin.

Dialogue 21

a. Are you Miss park?

b. No, I'm not. I'm Miss Kim.

a. I'm sorry. I must have you mixed up with

someone else.

Dialogue 22

a. How many are there in your family?

b. Five.

a. How many brothers and sisters do you

have?

b. I don't have any brothers, but I have two

sisters.

Dialogue 23

a. What does your father do for a living?

b. He works for the government.

a. How about your mother?

b. She is a housewife.

Dialogue 24

a. Do you mind if I ask your age?

b. Guess how old I am.

a. It's really hard to tell.

b. Just give it a try. How old do I look?

Dialogue 25

a. Where are you working these days?

b. I'm working at the Bank of Korea.

a. I've heard a great deal about that bank.

Dialogue 26

a. What was your first impression of Seoul?

b. The mountains are beautiful and people

are friendly.

a. What don't you like about Seoul?

b. Traffic is terrible and it's too crowded.

Dialogue 27

a. Have you ever been to America?

b. No, not yet. But I would like to go visit

my sister soon.

a. Which part of America does she live in?

b. In L.A.

SECTION 3

SAYING YOUR FAREWELLS

Dialogue 28

a. It was a lovely party.

b. Please give my regards to your family.

a. Okay. See you tomorrow at work.

b. Drive carefully.

Dialogue 29

a. Please excuse me, I'd better go now.

b. Well, please come back and see us again.

a. Perhaps we can get together at my place.

b. Okay. That will be great.

Dialogue 30

a. Good night, Mom and Dad.

b. Good night, kids. Did you brush your

teeth?

a. Yes, we did.

b. Make sure you go to bed right away.

Dialogue 31

a. I hope you have a good trip.

b. Let's get together when I get back.

a. Okay. Have fun and enjoy yourself!

b. I'll see you when I get back.

Dialogue 32

a. I shall miss all of you.

b. Please call us when you arrive.

a. Okay, I will

b. We'll be waiting for your call.

Dialogue 33

a. Let's meet more often.

b. Okay. That sounds great. When?

a. How about next week at the same place?

b. Okay. What time?

Dialogue 34

a. I'm sorry, but I've got to be on my way.

b. Do you have to go now?

a. Yes. I'm afraid so.

b. Will you come back soon?

Dialogue 35

a. Oh, is it ten already? I must go.

b. Do you have to work tomorrow?

a. Yes. I should be up by six in the morning

b. Then you need to get some sleep.

Dialogue 36

a. I'm sorry, but I'm in a hurry.

b. Why? Where are you going?

a. To the bank, before they close.

b. You still have thirty minutes.

Dialogue 37

a. Well, I guess I'd better be going.

b. Thank you for coming.

a. Thank you. I really enjoy dinner.

b. I'm very glad that you enjoyed it.

Dialogue 38

a. I enjoyed every minute of the party.

b. Thank you very much for the flowers.

a. Oh, it's my pleasure.

b. Good-bye. Let's keep in touch.

a. Sure. Next time I want to invite you to

my place.

Dialogue 39

a. I'm glad that you invited us to your

wedding.

b. Thank you for coming and for the lovely

gift.

a. It's not much. I hope you'll like it.

b. Oh, I'm sure we will. Thanks again.

Dialogue 40

a. Can't you stay a little longer?

b. We'd love to, but we should get going.

a. At least stay for dinner.

b. I'm sorry. We have another engagement.

Dialogue 41

a. Why are you in such a hurry?

b. Because I have to be home before three

o'clock.

a. Why?  What  time do  your  children get

home from school?

b. They will be home by 3:30.

Dialogue 42

a. Please keep in touch.

b. Okay. I'll call you soon.

a. Don't forget to say hello to your children.

SECTION 4

RISING TO THE OCCASION

Dialogue 43

a. Thank you for everything.

b. You're welcome.

a. It was very kind of you.

b. Please don't mention it.

Dialogue 44

a. How can I ever thank you?

b. That's okay. Don't worry about it.

a. Yes, but...

b. It's my pleasure.

Dialogue 45

a. Would you like to have some apple pie?

b. No, thank you. I'd better not.

a. Why not?

b. I'm on a diet.

Dialogue 46

a. Thank you very much for helping me.

b. It was my pleasure. You would do the

same for me, wouldn't you?

a. Of course I would.

Dialogue 47

a. Thanks a million, Mr. Choi, for what you

have done for me.

b. The pleasure is all mine.

a. You've been a great help.

b. I'm glad to have been able to help you.

Dialogue 48

a. Thank you for your hospitality.

b. I appreciate your visit.

a. How can I ever repay you?

b. Oh, come on, I just enjoyed your company

Dialogue 49

a. I had a wonderful time being with you.

b. I certainly enjoyed it, too.

a. I'm glad.

b. It couldn't have been better.

Dialogue 50

a. I had a very nice time and I thank you

very much.

b. I was glad to have your company.

a. I really enjoyed talking with you and

everything was delicious.

b. How kind of you to say so.

a. It couldn't have been better.

Dialogue 51

a. John, how can I ever thank you enough?

b. It's nothing. What are friends for?

a. I'm glad to have a friend like you.

Dialogue 52

a. Please forgive me for being late.

b. No problem.

a. I'm sorry to have kept you waiting

so long

b. Don't worry. You're forgiven.

Dialogue 53

a. I do apologize what I've done.

b. Don't blame yourself.

a. Then, who can I blame?

b. Just take it as a mistake.

Dialogue 54

a. I'm very sorry to hear about your father's

death.

b. Thank you for your condolences.

a. Is anything I can do for you?

b. Not at this time, but I'll let you know.

Dialogue 55

a. Oh, I'm sorry. I didn't recognize your

voice.

b. I know I sound different on the phone.

a. I hope I didn't offend you.

b. Not at all.

Dialogue 56

a. Excuse me. I had the wrong idea.

b. I'm sure your intentions were good.

a. I thought so.

b. Then what are you worrying about?

Dialogue 57

a. I don't want to get in your way.

b. Don't worry about it.

a. I know I'm the one who goofed it up.

b. Yeah, just a little.

Dialogue 58

a. It was my fault.

b. Oh no, it was my fault.

a. Don't blame yourself for something I did.

b. It wasn't your fault at all.

Dialogue 59

a. Please forgive me for my rude remarks.

b. I owe you an apology for my smart

remarks, too.

a. It just slipped out of my mouth.

b. Same here. I was very careless when I

said that.

Dialogue 60

a. Please forgive me for breaking my

promise.

b. Okay. But what happened yesterday?

a. My mother was very ill so we had to

take her to the hospital.

b. So how is she now?

SECTION 5

WORDS OF PRAISE AND

ENCOURAGEMENT

Dialogue 61

a. You did a nice job on dinner.

b. Did you like it?

a. Yes, very much.

b. Then I will cook for us from now on.

Dialogue 62

a. You look like a million bucks.

b. That's very nice of you to say so.

a. Do you exercise every day?

b. I try to.

Dialogue 63

a. Come on John, you can do it.

b. That's easier said than done.

a. Not really. You should try it first.

b. You think so?

Dialogue 64

a. What a cute baby! He has beautiful eyes.

b. Thank you for the compliment.

a. You're welcome. How old is he?

b. He's eleven months old now.

Dialogue 65

a. How do you keep in shape?

b. I stick to my diet and exercise a lot.

a. Ah, so that's your secret.

Dialogue 66

a. You came out beautiful in this picture.

b. Do you think so?

a. Yes, you look better than in real life.

b. That's what everybody tells me.

a. You're very photogenic.

Dialogue 67

a. Where did you get such a nice tan?

b. We went to the beach.

a. Did you go swimming?

b. No. Most of the time I just lay in the sun

Dialogue 68

a. What a lovely dress you're wearing!

b. Do you like it?

a. Yes. You look stunning in it.

b. I fell in love with it at first sight.

Dialogue 69

a. That tie goes well with your suit.

b. Thanks. It was a birthday gift from my

wife.

a. She has a good taste.

b. She knows how to make me look good.

Dialogue 70

a. How come you speak such good English?

b. I studied English in the States.

a. Where did you go to school?

b. I went to UCLA.

Dialogue 71

a. She is cut out to be a nurse.

b. I think so, too.

a. She is such a bright girl.

b. Yes, she has a memory like a computer.

Dialogue 72

a. I think you have an eye for fashion.

b. Maybe. I always try to look my best.

a. I think you should open a small boutique.

b. I would love to someday when I have the

money.

Dialogue 73

a. I hear you made vice-president of your

company.

b. Yes, I owe my success to my mother.

a. I envy your love for your mother.

b. She always supported my ideas, and

pushed me.

Dialogue 74

a. My father is very handy with tools.

b. What do you mean?

a. My father can fix just about anything.

b. Can he fix a car?

a. That depends on what's wrong with it.

b. I thought you said he can fix everything.

Dialogue 75

a. My brother has come along well.

b. Why? Was he a trouble maker?

a. Yes, when he was in high school.

b. So, you mean he's really straightened out?

Dialogue 76

a. You got a brand-new bike!

b. Yes, my Dad bought it for my birthday.

a. When was your birthday?

b. It was yesterday.

Dialogue 77

a. What a gorgeous girl friend you have!

b. Thanks.

a. Where did you meet her?

b. Oh, we met at a party.

Dialogue 78

a. You have a lovely home.

b. Thank you. We love it, too.

a. Who designed your fire place?

b. We did everything ourselves.

Dialogue 79

a. Don't worry. I'll stick by you.

b. Thank you for being my friend.

a. That's what friends are for.

b. How can I ever repay you?

Dialogue 80

a. Don't give up so easily.

b. I don't think I can do it anymore.

a. Don't ever say that in front of me.

b. Thank you for the vote of confidence.

Dialogue 81

a. Everything will be fine.

b. I sure hope so.

a. God will be with you.

b. Would you pray for me?

Dialogue 82

a. Can I count on you?

b. Sure, you can count on me.

a. Don't let me down.

b. I said I can help you with that problem.

Dialogue 83

a. Come to me if you are ever in any

difficulty

b. Thank you for your offer.

a. You bet.

b. I'm sure you will be a big help someday.

Dialogue 84

a. You've got lots of time to improve your

English.

b. I know, but I don't have the patience.

a. Just think positive and have faith in

yourself. You'll never know until you try.

All it takes is a little courage.

b. Thank you for your advice.

Dialogue 85

a. I know you can deal with your problem.

b. I did it before.

a. See, I know you can do it.

b. But this time I'm not sure.

Dialogue 86

a. Don't be upset.

b. How can I not be upset?

a. But that's life, isn't it?

b. That's easy for you to say. You're not in

my shoes.

Dialogue 87

a. It's not as hard as you think it is.

b. That's easy for you to say.

a. I bet you'll be very successful.

b. I sure hope so.

a. Just don't give up hope.

b. I don't want to be a failure in my life.

Dialogue 88

a. Don't let that bother you.

b. How can you act like there's no

tomorrow?

a. That's not what I mean.

b. Then what?

Dialogue 89

a. Don't worry too much.

b. How can I not worry?

a. Just don't put yourself down so much.

b. I try not to.

Dialogue 90

a. I wish you the best of luck.

b. Thank you.

a. I'm sure you'll win this one.

b. Cross your fingers for me, will you?

a. Sure.

b. Thanks a lot. I won't forget this.

SECTION 6

WORDS OF ADVICE

Dialogue 91

a. Why don't you stop saying bad things

about me?

b. Come on, don't lose your temper.

a. Why do you always tease people?

b. Don't take it so seriously. I was just

joking.

a. You carry your jokes too far.

Dialogue 92

a. Don't be afraid of making mistakes.

b. I'm not. I'm just trying to be careful.

a. That's good.

Dialogue 93

a. It's not going to work.

b. How can you say that before you try?

a. I'm telling you, it's not going to work.

b. Please don't let me down.

Dialogue 94

a. I have to work hard to make up for lost

time.

b. Don't be so hard on yourself.

a. If the table were turned, you'd see how I

feel right now.

Dialogue 95

a. Can you keep it to yourself?

b. No problem. My lips are sealed.

a. I should have known I could trust you.

b. Don't fret. I won't spill the beans.

Dialogue 96

a. I know you're very upset right now.

b. I should have taken a closer look at him

before.

a. Maybe you should pocket your pride and

talk to him when you're calm.

Dialogue 97

a. Tell me word for word what happened to

you.

b. Nothing. Don't worry about me.

a. Don't be so uptight.

b. If I tell you, can you keep a straight face?

a. Sure, is it funny?

b. No, but, you might think it's stupid.

Dialogue 98

a. Did you look it up in the dictionary?

b. Not yet.

a. You should look it up. It's very important

for you to know what this word means.

Dialogue 99

a. Don't mess with me, or you'll be sorry.

b. What are you trying to tell me?

a. Forget it!

b. No. Tell me!

Dialogue 100

a. You cannot have it both ways.

b. Why not?

a. Because that is how it works in life.

b. Well, I'll try again anyway.

Dialogue 101

a. He gave me lots of advice.

b. Really? Like what?

a. The first thing he said was to be all I can

be.

b. Can I give you a piece of my mind, too?

a. Sure, I need all the advice I can get.

Dialogue 102

a. Shame on you for giving up so soon.

b. I went by the book, but...

a. Everything needs time.

b. Maybe I just don't have enough patience.

Dialogue 103

a. Go easy on him.

b. But he's been walking on thin ice.

a. I know. He just doesn't understand what

he's doing.

b. OK. I'll give him one more chance to

straighten up.

Dialogue 104

a. Don't rely on him too much. He's a poor

risk.

b. Why? What did he do to you?

a. He has a bad reputation.

b. The man next door told me the same

thing.

Dialogue 105

a. You've got to give up smoking.

b. I know. I have been trying for a long

time.

a. I know a good method of how to quit

smoking.

b. Good! What is it? I might try again.

Dialogue 106

a. Please don't raise your voice at me.

b. Give him what he wants and be done

with it.

a. But he always gets his way.

b. I have a better idea how to handle him.

a. What's that?

Dialogue 107

a. I don't mean to be nosy, but you were

wrong.

b. Do you think so?

a. Don't spoil your child like that.

b. Yes, but it is so hard not to.

Dialogue 108

a. Be careful. She'll make you crazy.

b. How much did she have?

a. Too much.

b. Whose party did you guys go to?

a. Mr. park's going away party.

SECTION 7

ASKING FAVORS

Dialogue 109

a. Would you do me a favor?

b. Sure, if it's something I'm capable of.

b. Gee, I don't know. When is it?

b. Let me check first.

b. Not at all.

b. Sorry, we don't have a Korean typewriter.

a. Would you loan me a hundred bucks?

b. Let me check my checkbook first.

a. Can you let me know as soon as possible?

b. How about tomorrow?

a. Would you give me a lift?

b. Which one?

b. Oh, that one! Have you been there before?

Dialogue 120

a. Tell me about your trip.

b. It was great.

a. How long were you away?

b. About a week. It was too short.

Dialogue 121

a. Will you have a beer with me?

b. Sure, what kind should we get?

a. How about Budweiser?

b. Fine with me.

Dialogue 122

a. Could you watch the baby while I go pick

up my son?

b. Sure, what time do you need me?

a. Sometime this afternoon, around 3 p.m.

Dialogue 123

a. Would you go with me to a concert?

b. Sure, when?

a. This weekend. Please don't let me down

again.

b. Don't worry. I'm going to write it down.

a. You said that last time.

b. Trust me. I'll remember this time.

SIGNS AND POSTERS

b. Why?
